# Test redundancy

Generated by `go run ./cmd/testtiming -redundancy` under the tags `integration,tmux`.
Each test ran alone under one coverage binary per package, and a candidate has every covered block also covered by another test of its package.
A candidate is evidence, not a verdict: coverage blocks do not see assertions.
Only the removable set may be deleted together; the reading rules are in `pattern/PATTERN-test-economy.md`.
